| 62b7639a5a68:384 | Legislation: Sozialgesetzbuch IV and VI. Ceilings, rates and the amounts for compulsorily insured self-employed people: `de-social-contributions`. | Exactwordingretained currentLF384, Section 8: Social Security Optimization |
| 62b7639a5a68:388 | **Self-Employed Social Security table** | Exactwordingretained currentLF388, Self-Employed Social Security |
| 62b7639a5a68:390 | \| Category \| Detail \| | Exactwordingretained currentLF390, Self-Employed Social Security |
| 62b7639a5a68:392 | \| Mandatory cases \| § 2 SGB VI: teachers, educators and carers with no insured employee; midwives; sea pilots; artists and publicists; home-based traders; skippers; craftspeople in the Handwerksrolle; and persons with no insured employee working essentially for one client \| | Exactwordingretained currentLF392, Self-Employed Social Security |
| 62b7639a5a68:393 | \| Everyone else \| Not compulsorily insured; voluntary insurance (§ 7 SGB VI), or compulsory insurance on application within five years of starting (§ 4 SGB VI). A Versorgungswerk follows state law, not the federal pages \| | Exactwordingretained currentLF393, Self-Employed Social Security |
| 62b7639a5a68:394 | \| One client is not false self-employment \| That person is self-employed and pension-insured (§ 2 no. 9 SGB VI); false self-employment means the work is in truth employment (§ 7(1) SGB IV) \| | Exactwordingretained currentLF394, Self-Employed Social Security |
| 62b7639a5a68:396 | Pages: https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/sgb_6/__2.html and https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/sgb_4/__7.html | Exactwordingretained currentLF396, Self-Employed Social Security |

| 62b7639a5a68:402 | \| Strategy \| Detail \| | Exactwordingretained currentLF402, Optimization Strategies |
| 62b7639a5a68:404 | \| Voluntary pension contributions \| Special expenses under § 10(1) no. 2(a) EStG, only up to the one cap in Section 9. Amounts: `de-social-contributions` \| | Exactwordingretained currentLF404, Optimization Strategies |
| 62b7639a5a68:405 | \| Versorgungswerk \| In the same sentence as statutory contributions, so it shares that one cap \| | Exactwordingretained currentLF405, Optimization Strategies |
| 62b7639a5a68:406 | \| Krankenversicherung \| Basic health and care contributions are special expenses; for private cover only the part comparable to statutory cover. The choice is not a tax question \| | Exactwordingretained currentLF406, Optimization Strategies |
| 62b7639a5a68:407 | \| Beitragsbemessungsgrenze \| Contributions stop at each branch's ceiling; income above it adds none \| | Exactwordingretained currentLF407, Optimization Strategies |

| 62b7639a5a68:415 | \| Feature \| Detail \| | Exactwordingretained currentLF237, Investitionsabzugsbetrag (IAB): § 7g EStG |
| 62b7639a5a68:417 | \| What counts \| Statutory pension, farmers' fund and comparable professional funds, and an own funded contract paying only a lifelong monthly annuity from age 62, with rights that cannot be inherited, sold or paid as a lump sum \| | Exactwordingretained currentLF417, Basisrente (Rürup-Rente): § 10 Abs. 1 Nr. 2 EStG |
| 62b7639a5a68:418 | \| Deduction limit \| Full contributions since 2023, up to one cap shared by statutory, Versorgungswerk and Basisrente contributions. The statute prints no euro amount; the 2026 amounts are below \| | Exactwordingretained currentLF418, Basisrente (Rürup-Rente): § 10 Abs. 1 Nr. 2 EStG |
| 62b7639a5a68:419 | \| Later and locked \| Taxed when paid (§ 22 EStG), at a share fixed by the year the pension starts and higher for each later year. The money is locked in \| | Exactwordingretained currentLF419, Basisrente (Rürup-Rente): § 10 Abs. 1 Nr. 2 EStG |

| 62b7639a5a68:441 | - **Günstigerprüfung.** On application all investment income of the year is taxed at the tariff instead, where the total tax including surcharges is lower (§ 32d(6) EStG). Gains, funds and exit tax: `de-capital-gains`. Freelancer income tax: `de-einkommensteuer-freelancer`. | Exactwordingretained currentLF441, Vermögenswirksame Leistungen (VWL) and Other |

| 62b7639a5a68:455 | \| Risk \| Detail \| | Exactwordingretained currentLF455, Section 10: Red Lines |
| 62b7639a5a68:457 | \| § 42 AO \| An inappropriate arrangement giving a tax advantage the law does not provide for is an abuse unless non-tax reasons that matter overall are proven \| | Exactwordingretained currentLF457, Section 10: Red Lines |
| 62b7639a5a68:458 | \| Deferral models (§ 15b EStG) \| Losses from a model aimed at early losses reduce only later income from the same source \| | Exactwordingretained currentLF458, Section 10: Red Lines |
| 62b7639a5a68:459 | \| Scheinselbständigkeit \| Working under instructions and inside the other's work organisation (§ 7(1) SGB IV). Past periods: refer \| | Exactwordingretained currentLF459, Section 10: Red Lines |
| 62b7639a5a68:460 | \| Deduction without the investment \| Reversed in the year claimed, with interest at the rate below from 15 months after that year \| | Exactwordingretained currentLF460, Section 10: Red Lines |

| 62b7639a5a68:462 | \| Hidden profit distribution \| It does not reduce the GmbH's income (§ 8(3) KStG), and the statute does not define it \| | Exactwordingretained currentLF462, Section 10: Red Lines |

| 62b7639a5a68:465 | \| Home office claims \| Room costs only where the room is the centre of all business and professional activity \| | Exactwordingretained currentLF465, Section 10: Red Lines |

| 62b7639a5a68:477 | Statutory due dates; one on a weekend or holiday runs to the next working day (§ 108(3) AO). VAT advance returns are due by the tenth day after each return period (§ 18(1) UStG), and the § 46 UStDV extension moves every VAT date by a month. | Exactwordingretained currentLF477, Section 11: Annual Tax Planning Calendar |
| 62b7639a5a68:479 | **Annual Tax Planning Calendar table** | Exactwordingretained currentLF479, Section 11: Annual Tax Planning Calendar |
| 62b7639a5a68:481 | \| Month \| Action \| | Exactwordingretained currentLF481, Section 11: Annual Tax Planning Calendar |
| 62b7639a5a68:483 | \| January \| **10 January**: VAT return for December or the fourth quarter. Check open investment deductions \| | Exactwordingretained currentLF483, Section 11: Annual Tax Planning Calendar |
| 62b7639a5a68:484 | \| February \| **15 February**: trade tax prepayment. **End of February**: adviser returns for the year before last \| | Exactwordingretained currentLF484, Section 11: Annual Tax Planning Calendar |
| 62b7639a5a68:485 | \| March to June \| **10 March** and **10 June**: income tax prepayments. **15 May**: trade tax prepayment. **10 April**: VAT return for the first quarter. Compare single and joint assessment before filing \| | Exactwordingretained currentLF485, Section 11: Annual Tax Planning Calendar |
| 62b7639a5a68:486 | \| July \| **10 July**: VAT return for the second quarter or June. **31 July**: returns for last year without an adviser \| | Exactwordingretained currentLF486, Section 11: Annual Tax Planning Calendar |
| 62b7639a5a68:487 | \| August, September \| **15 August**: trade tax prepayment. **10 September**: income tax prepayment \| | Exactwordingretained currentLF487, Section 11: Annual Tax Planning Calendar |
| 62b7639a5a68:488 | \| October \| **10 October**: VAT return for the third quarter or September. Check turnover against the small-business limit \| | Exactwordingretained currentLF488, Section 11: Annual Tax Planning Calendar |
| 62b7639a5a68:489 | \| November \| **10 November**: VAT return. **15 November**: trade tax prepayment. Plan pension contributions \| | Exactwordingretained currentLF489, Section 11: Annual Tax Planning Calendar |
| 62b7639a5a68:490 | \| December \| **10 December**: income tax prepayment. **31 December**: last day for payments to count in an EÜR, apart from the regularly recurring items in Section 6, for assets to be bought rather than ordered, and for a marriage to open joint assessment for the year \| | Exactwordingretained currentLF490, Section 11: Annual Tax Planning Calendar |
| 62b7639a5a68:494 | The legacy examples used made-up incomes and computed a saving at an assumed rate. No official page prints those numbers and this library does not compute tax, so each is kept as the mechanism in words. For a real case apply the tariff formula of § 32a EStG once with the item and once without it, and never multiply a deduction by a top rate. | Exactwordingretained currentLF494, Section 12: Cash Impact Examples |
| 62b7639a5a68:498 | **Example 1.** Joint assessment adds both incomes, taxes half at the tariff and doubles it, and doubles the surcharge limit, the saver's allowance and the pension cap. Work both options. | Exactwordingretained currentLF498, Example 1: Ehegattensplitting |
| 62b7639a5a68:502 | **Example 2.** Basisrente contributions, with statutory or Versorgungswerk ones, are special expenses up to the 2026 cap in Section 9 and cut taxable income by the amount paid, no more. | Exactwordingretained currentLF502, Example 2: Rürup-Rente Contribution |
| 62b7639a5a68:506 | **Example 3.** Part of the expected cost is deducted early; on purchase the same amount is added back and taken off the asset's cost, so depreciation runs on less. Over the asset's life the deductions equal its cost: the gain is timing. | Exactwordingretained currentLF506, Example 3: IAB + Sonderabschreibung |
| 62b7639a5a68:510 | **Example 4.** A laptop below the immediate write-off limit, measured without the input VAT in the price, is deducted in full in the purchase year unless the pool was chosen for that year. | Exactwordingretained currentLF510, Example 4: GWG Immediate Write-Off |
| 62b7639a5a68:514 | **Example 5.** Qualifying days times the day rate in the § 4 table, never above the yearly cap there. Keep a record of the days. | Exactwordingretained currentLF514, Example 5: Homeoffice-Pauschale |
| 62b7639a5a68:518 | 1. Fix the facts: legal form, trade or liberal profession (§ 15 and § 18 EStG), and EÜR or balance sheet (§ 4(3) EStG). https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/estg/__4.html | Exactwordingretained currentLF518, The method, step by step |
| 62b7639a5a68:519 | 2. Work out the expected profit and test it against the § 7g profit limit before any investment deduction or special depreciation. https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/estg/__7g.html | Exactwordingretained currentLF519, The method, step by step |
| 62b7639a5a68:520 | 3. Go through the deductions in Section 3, checking each limit and the separate record § 4(7) EStG demands. https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/estg/__9.html | Exactwordingretained currentLF520, The method, step by step |
| 62b7639a5a68:521 | 4. Per fixed asset: low-value asset or pool first, one choice for the whole year (§ 6(2a) EStG), then the depreciation method and its date window. https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/estg/__6.html | Exactwordingretained currentLF521, The method, step by step |
| 62b7639a5a68:522 | 5. Losses: offset within the year, then the carry-back or its waiver, then the carry-forward (§ 10d EStG). https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/estg/__10d.html | Exactwordingretained currentLF522, The method, step by step |
| 62b7639a5a68:523 | 6. Special expenses in the year paid, and the choice between single and joint assessment in the return (§ 10 and § 26 EStG). https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/estg/__10.html | Exactwordingretained currentLF523, The method, step by step |
| 62b7639a5a68:524 | 7. VAT: test both small-business limits before waiving the rule, knowing the waiver binds for at least five years. https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/ustg_1980/__19.html | Exactwordingretained currentLF524, The method, step by step |
| 62b7639a5a68:525 | 8. Test every idea against § 42 AO, write down the non-tax reasons, and refer before any change of structure; a binding ruling can be asked for beforehand, for a fee (§ 89 AO). https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/ao_1977/__89.html | Exactwordingretained currentLF525, The method, step by step |
| 62b7639a5a68:529 | - What is the legal form and kind of income, and is profit worked out by EÜR or by balance sheet? | Exactwordingretained currentLF529, Ask the client first |
| 62b7639a5a68:530 | - What is the expected profit this year before any investment deduction, what was last year's, and how much must be withdrawn to live on? | Exactwordingretained currentLF530, Ask the client first |
| 62b7639a5a68:531 | - Is the client married or in a civil partnership, both fully liable to tax in Germany and living together, and does the spouse work in the business? | Exactwordingretained currentLF531, Ask the client first |
| 62b7639a5a68:532 | - Which assets will be bought in the next three years, when, and how much will each be used privately? | Exactwordingretained currentLF532, Ask the client first |
| 62b7639a5a68:533 | - VAT: last year's total turnover, this year's so far, and are large purchases planned? | Exactwordingretained currentLF533, Ask the client first |
| 62b7639a5a68:534 | - How is the client insured for old age and health, and does the client work essentially for one client? | Exactwordingretained currentLF534, Ask the client first |
| 62b7639a5a68:538 | - Anything cross-border: a home, spouse, income, clients, staff, company or assets outside Germany; treaties; foreign tax credits; controlled foreign company rules; transfer pricing (`germany-transfer-pricing`). | Exactwordingretained currentLF538, When to refuse or refer |
| 62b7639a5a68:539 | - Exit tax: leaving Germany, or giving shares abroad, with a shareholding within § 17 EStG or a large fund holding (§ 6 AStG). See `de-capital-gains`, and refer before the move. | Exactwordingretained currentLF539, When to refuse or refer |
| 62b7639a5a68:540 | - Any arrangement whose main purpose is a tax advantage, any model with planned early losses, any structure the client cannot explain by non-tax reasons. | Exactwordingretained currentLF540, When to refuse or refer |
| 62b7639a5a68:541 | - Choosing or changing the legal form: a GmbH, UG or GmbH & Co. KG, the § 1a KStG option, contributing a business under § 20 UmwStG, holdings, tax groups. | Exactwordingretained currentLF541, When to refuse or refer |
| 62b7639a5a68:542 | - A § 34a EStG application, which starts a record that runs for years and ends in a follow-up tax. | Exactwordingretained currentLF542, When to refuse or refer |
| 62b7639a5a68:543 | - Pay, loans, rent and pension promises between a GmbH and its owner: the hidden profit distribution tests are case law. | Exactwordingretained currentLF543, When to refuse or refer |
| 62b7639a5a68:544 | - Real estate structuring: property companies, splitting a price between land and building, private sales of property, real estate transfer tax, a shorter useful life for a building. | Exactwordingretained currentLF544, When to refuse or refer |
| 62b7639a5a68:545 | - Inheritance and gift planning, and handing the business on: the inheritance and gift tax act was not read for this Guide. | Exactwordingretained currentLF545, When to refuse or refer |
| 62b7639a5a68:546 | - Selling or closing the business, or selling shares: the § 16(4) and § 17 EStG allowances, the § 34 EStG reduced rate, the seven-year rule of § 22 UmwStG. | Exactwordingretained currentLF546, When to refuse or refer |
| 62b7639a5a68:547 | - Employing family members: the contract, the pay level, the social insurance status, and any suspected false self-employment. | Exactwordingretained currentLF547, When to refuse or refer |
| 62b7639a5a68:548 | - Electric and hybrid company cars other than the quarter rule in Section 4, and any car whose private use is disputed. | Exactwordingretained currentLF548, When to refuse or refer |
| 62b7639a5a68:549 | - Limited partners' losses (§ 15a EStG), the interest barrier (§ 4h EStG), farming and forestry. | Exactwordingretained currentLF549, When to refuse or refer |
| 62b7639a5a68:550 | - Tax arrears, deferral, enforcement, voluntary disclosure and any criminal tax matter. | Exactwordingretained currentLF550, When to refuse or refer |
| 62b7639a5a68:551 | - The exact church tax rate for a client: it is set by state church tax rules, not on the federal pages. | Exactwordingretained currentLF551, When to refuse or refer |
| 62b7639a5a68:552 | - The choice of a pension, insurance or investment product, and private versus statutory health cover. | Exactwordingretained currentLF552, When to refuse or refer |
| 62b7639a5a68:553 | - Computing the client's tax or a saving. Payroll: `de-payroll`. Trade tax: `de-trade-tax`. VAT returns: `germany-vat-return`. Bookkeeping: `germany-bookkeeping`. Rental property: `de-rental-income`. Formation: `germany-formation`. | Exactwordingretained currentLF553, When to refuse or refer |
| 62b7639a5a68:557 | Each paragraph is linked where it is used. Laws read: Einkommensteuergesetz, Abgabenordnung, Körperschaftsteuergesetz, Gewerbesteuergesetz, Solidaritätszuschlaggesetz, Umsatzsteuergesetz, Sozialgesetzbuch IV and VI and the Fifth Capital Formation Act, at https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/ Also the ministry's 2026 EÜR instructions (Section 4) and tax booklet (Section 1), and the pension insurer's 2026 pages (Sections 8 and 9). | Exactwordingretained currentLF557, Sources |
